          imgBoxH = width / arImg;
          imgY = y + (height - imgBoxH) / 2;
        } else {
          imgBoxH = height;
          imgBoxW = height * arImg;
          imgX = x + (width - imgBoxW) / 2;
        }
      } else if (imgW > 0 && imgH > 0 && width > 0 && height > 0) {
        const arImg = imgW / imgH;
        const arBox = width / height;
        if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
          // Image is taller than container box: crop top & bottom (object-fit: cover)
          const cropY = (1.0 - arImg / arBox) / 2.0;
          const cropVal = Math.round(cropY * 65536);
          cropAttr = ` croptop="${cropVal}f" cropbottom="${cropVal}f"`;
        } else if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
          // Image is wider than container box: crop left & right
          const cropX = (1.0 - arBox / arImg) / 2.0;
          const cropVal = Math.round(cropX * 65536);
          cropAttr = ` cropleft="${cropVal}f" cropright="${cropVal}f"`;
        }
      }

      const imgStyle = `position:absolute;left:0;top:0;margin-left:${imgX.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;margin-top:${imgY.toFixed(3)}pt;width:${imgBoxW.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;height:${imgBoxH.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;z-index:${z};mso-position-horizontal-relative:page;mso-position-vertical-relative:page;mso-wrap-style:none${rotStyle}`;

      return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:shape id="${shapeId}"
             ${altAttr}
             ${titleAttr}
             style="${imgStyle}"
             filled="f"
             stroked="f">
      <v:imagedata r:id="${relId}"${cropAttr} o:title="${escapeXml(elem.name || "image")}"/>
    </v:shape>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
    }

    // 3. Card or Text
    const isText = elem.type === "text" || (elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);
    const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor && elem.style.fillColor !== "none";
    const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

    const filledAttr = hasFill ? 'filled="t"' : 'filled="f"';
    const fillcolorAttr = hasFill ? `fillcolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}"` : "";

    const strokedAttr = hasStroke ? 'stroked="t"' : 'stroked="f"';
    const strokecolorAttr = hasStroke ? `strokecolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor)}"` : "";
    const strokeweightAttr = hasStroke
      ? `strokeweight="${(elem.style.strokeWidth || 1).toFixed(2)}pt"`
      : "";

    // Corner radius or Oval
    const isOval = elem.type === "oval";
    const isRound = !isOval && !!elem.style.cornerRadius && elem.style.cornerRadius > 0;
    const tag = isOval ? "v:oval" : isRound ? "v:roundrect" : "v:rect";
    const minDim = Math.max(1e-3, Math.min(width, height));
    const arcsizeAttr = isRound
      ? `arcsize="${Math.min(0.5, (elem.style.cornerRadius || 0) / minDim).toFixed(3)}"`
      : "";

    let textboxContentXml = "";
    if (isText && elem.textRuns) {
      const jcVal = elem.paragraphAlign || "left";
      const runsXml: string[] = [];

      for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
        const { ascii, eastAsia } = resolveEastAsiaFont(run.fontFamily);
        const fontAscii = escapeXml(ascii);
        const fontEastAsia = escapeXml(eastAsia);
        const fontSizeHalfPt = Math.round((run.fontSizePt || 10) * 2);
        const colorHex = cleanHex(run.color, "000000");

        const boldXml = (run.fontWeight || 400) >= 600 ? "<w:b/>" : "";
        const italicXml = run.italic ? "<w:i/>" : "";
        const uXml = run.underline ? '<w:u w:val="single"/>' : "";
        const strikeXml = run.strike ? "<w:strike/>" : "";
        const letterSpacingTwips =
          typeof run.letterSpacingPt === "number" && run.letterSpacingPt !== 0
            ? ptToTwip(run.letterSpacingPt)
            : 0;
        const letterSpacingXml =
          letterSpacingTwips !== 0 ? `<w:spacing w:val="${letterSpacingTwips}"/>` : "";

        const lines = run.text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
        const textParts = lines.map((l) => `<w:t xml:space="preserve">${escapeXml(l)}</w:t>`);
        const textXml = textParts.join("<w:br/>");

        runsXml.push(`<w:r>
  <w:rPr>
    <w:rFonts w:ascii="${fontAscii}" w:hAnsi="${fontAscii}" w:eastAsia="${fontEastAsia}" w:cs="${fontAscii}"/>
    <w:sz w:val="${fontSizeHalfPt}"/>
    <w:szCs w:val="${fontSizeHalfPt}"/>
    <w:color w:val="${colorHex}"/>
    ${letterSpacingXml}
    ${boldXml}
    ${italicXml}
    ${uXml}
    ${strikeXml}
  </w:rPr>
  ${textXml}
</w:r>`);
      }

      const pad = elem.innerPaddingPt || {};
      const padTop = pad.top ?? 0;
      const padRight = pad.right ?? 0;
      const padBottom = pad.bottom ?? 0;
      const padLeft = pad.left ?? 0;
      const insetAttr = `inset="${padLeft.toFixed(2)}pt,${padTop.toFixed(2)}pt,${padRight.toFixed(2)}pt,${padBottom.toFixed(2)}pt"`;

      const firstLineHeight = elem.textRuns.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt)?.lineHeightPt;
      const maxFontSize = Math.max(...elem.textRuns.map((r) => r.fontSizePt || 12));
      const isPureText = elem.type === "text" && !hasFill && !hasStroke;

      // Auto-fit height for pure text elements (or elements with auto-resize), fixed height for cards/buttons
      // For vertically centered elements (v-text-anchor:middle), keep fixed height so Word anchors to middle
      const shouldFitHeight =
        elem.verticalAlign !== "center" &&
        (isPureText ||
          elem.textAutoResize === "HEIGHT" ||
          elem.textAutoResize === "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT");
      const fitTextAttr =
        shouldFitHeight && !hasFill && !hasStroke
          ? "mso-fit-shape-to-text:t"
          : "mso-fit-shape-to-text:f";

      const isTightLine = !!(firstLineHeight && firstLineHeight < maxFontSize * 1.25);
      const lineRule = isTightLine ? "atLeast" : "exact";

      const spacingXml = firstLineHeight
        ? `<w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0" w:line="${Math.round(firstLineHeight * 20)}" w:lineRule="${lineRule}"/>`
        : `<w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0" w:line="240" w:lineRule="auto"/>`;

      const vAnchor = elem.verticalAlign === "center" ? "v-text-anchor:middle" : elem.verticalAlign === "bottom" ? "v-text-anchor:bottom" : "v-text-anchor:top";
      textboxContentXml = `
      <v:textbox style="${fitTextAttr};mso-next-textbox:none;${vAnchor}" ${insetAttr}>
        <w:txbxContent>
          <w:p>
            <w:pPr>
              <w:jc w:val="${jcVal}"/>
              ${spacingXml}
              <w:contextualSpacing w:val="0"/>
              <w:autoSpaceDE w:val="0"/>
              <w:autoSpaceDN w:val="0"/>
            </w:pPr>
            ${runsXml.join("\n            ")}
          </w:p>
        </w:txbxContent>
      </v:textbox>`;
    }

    const dashChild = hasStroke && elem.style.strokeDashed ? '<v:stroke dashstyle="dash"/>' : "";
    const opacityChild =
      hasFill && elem.style.fillOpacity !== undefined && elem.style.fillOpacity < 1
        ? `<v:fill opacity="${elem.style.fillOpacity.toFixed(2)}"/>`
        : "";

    return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <${tag} id="${shapeId}"
            ${altAttr}
            ${titleAttr}
            style="${baseStyle}"
            ${filledAttr}
            ${fillcolorAttr}
            ${strokedAttr}
            ${strokecolorAttr}
            ${strokeweightAttr}
            ${arcsizeAttr}>${dashChild}${opacityChild}${textboxContentXml}
    </${tag}>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
  }
}


================================================================================
File: src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.ts
================================================================================
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasResource,
  ptToHwpu,
} from "../types.js";
import { SvgPath } from "../geometry/svgPathParser.js";

export interface HwpxCanvasRendererOptions {
  creator?: string;
}

function escapeXml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&apos;")
    .replace(/[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F]/g, "");
}

function cleanHex(color?: string, fallback: string = "000000"): string {
  if (!color) return fallback;
  return color.replace("#", "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

interface CharPrKey {
  fontId: number;
  fontSizePt: number;
  colorHex: string;
  bold: boolean;
  italic: boolean;
  underline: boolean;
  strike: boolean;
  letterSpacingPct: number;
}

interface ParaPrEntry {
  id: number;
  horizontal: "LEFT" | "CENTER" | "RIGHT" | "JUSTIFY";
  spacingType: "PERCENT" | "FIXED";
  spacingValue: number;
  spacingUnit: "PERCENT" | "HWPUNIT";
}

export class HwpxCanvasRenderer {
  private creator: string;

  constructor(options?: HwpxCanvasRendererOptions) {
    this.creator = options?.creator || "Figma2Word Canvas Engine";
  }

  public async buildZip(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<JSZip> {
    const zip = new JSZip();

    // 1. mimetype (MUST be first entry, STORED uncompressed, 19 bytes)
    zip.file("mimetype", "application/hwp+zip", {
      compression: "STORE",
    });

    // 2. version.xml & settings.xml
    zip.file("version.xml", this.buildVersionXml());
    zip.file("settings.xml", this.buildSettingsXml());

    // 3. META-INF/container.xml
    zip.file("META-INF/container.xml", this.buildContainerXml());

    // 4. BinData/
    const imageBinMap = new Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>();
    let binCounter = 1;

    for (const [resId, res] of Object.entries(doc.resources)) {
      const binId = `BIN${binCounter.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`;
      const isJpeg =
        res.mimeType === "image/jpeg" ||
        res.mimeType === "image/jpg" ||
        (res.data && res.data.length >= 3 && res.data[0] === 0xff && res.data[1] === 0xd8);
      const ext = isJpeg ? "jpg" : "png";
      const mimeType = isJpeg ? "image/jpeg" : "image/png";
      const binFileName = `${binId}.${ext}`;
      imageBinMap.set(resId, { binId, ext, mimeType });
      zip.file(`BinData/${binFileName}`, res.data);
      binCounter++;
    }

    // 5. META-INF/manifest.xml & Contents/content.hpf
    zip.file("META-INF/manifest.xml", this.buildManifestXml(imageBinMap));
    zip.file("Contents/content.hpf", this.buildContentHpf(doc, imageBinMap));

    // 6. Dynamic Font Registry (7 OWPML languages)
    const fontRegistry = new Map<string, number>();
    fontRegistry.set("맑은 고딕", 0); // Default fallback at index 0

    for (const page of doc.pages) {
      for (const elem of page.elements) {
        if (elem.textRuns) {
          for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
            const font = run.fontFamily?.trim() || "맑은 고딕";
            if (!fontRegistry.has(font)) {
              fontRegistry.set(font, fontRegistry.size);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Dynamic style registries
    const charPrRegistry = new Map<string, number>(); // serializedKey -> id
    const charPrList: { id: number; key: CharPrKey }[] = [];

    const getCharPrId = (run: CanvasTextRun): number => {
      const font = run.fontFamily?.trim() || "맑은 고딕";
      const fontId = fontRegistry.get(font) ?? 0;
      const fontSizePt = Math.round((run.fontSizePt || 10) * 10) / 10;
      const letterSpacingPct =
        run.letterSpacingPt !== undefined && fontSizePt > 0
          ? Math.max(-50, Math.min(50, Math.round((run.letterSpacingPt / fontSizePt) * 100)))
          : 0;
      const key: CharPrKey = {
        fontId,
        fontSizePt,
        colorHex: cleanHex(run.color, "000000"),
        bold: (run.fontWeight || 400) >= 600,
        italic: !!run.italic,
        underline: !!run.underline,
        strike: !!run.strike,
        letterSpacingPct,
      };
      const serialized = `${fontId}_${key.fontSizePt}_${key.colorHex}_${key.bold}_${key.italic}_${key.underline}_${key.strike}_${key.letterSpacingPct}`;
      if (charPrRegistry.has(serialized)) {
        return charPrRegistry.get(serialized)!;
      }
      const newId = charPrList.length;
      charPrRegistry.set(serialized, newId);
      charPrList.push({ id: newId, key });
      return newId;
    };

    const paraPrList: ParaPrEntry[] = [
      { id: 0, horizontal: "LEFT", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 1, horizontal: "CENTER", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 2, horizontal: "RIGHT", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 3, horizontal: "JUSTIFY", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
    ];
    const paraPrRegistry = new Map<string, number>([
      ["LEFT_PERCENT_160", 0],
      ["CENTER_PERCENT_160", 1],
      ["RIGHT_PERCENT_160", 2],
      ["JUSTIFY_PERCENT_160", 3],
    ]);

    const getParaPrId = (
      align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify",
      lineHeightPt?: number
    ): number => {
      const horizontal: "LEFT" | "CENTER" | "RIGHT" | "JUSTIFY" =
        align === "center"
          ? "CENTER"
          : align === "right"
          ? "RIGHT"
          : align === "justify"
          ? "JUSTIFY"
          : "LEFT";
      if (!lineHeightPt || lineHeightPt <= 0) {
        return paraPrRegistry.get(`${horizontal}_PERCENT_160`) ?? 0;
      }
      const spacingValue = Math.max(100, Math.round(lineHeightPt * 100));
      const key = `${horizontal}_FIXED_${spacingValue}`;
      if (paraPrRegistry.has(key)) {
        return paraPrRegistry.get(key)!;
      }
      const newId = paraPrList.length;
      paraPrRegistry.set(key, newId);
      paraPrList.push({
        id: newId,
        horizontal,
        spacingType: "FIXED",
        spacingValue,
        spacingUnit: "HWPUNIT",
      });
      return newId;
    };

    // 7. Contents/section0.xml (builds shapes & populates charPrRegistry / paraPrRegistry)
    const section0Xml = this.buildSection0Xml(doc, imageBinMap, getCharPrId, getParaPrId);
    zip.file("Contents/section0.xml", section0Xml);

    // 8. Contents/header.xml (outputs registered fontfaces, charPr & paraPr styles)
    zip.file("Contents/header.xml", this.buildHeaderXml(charPrList, fontRegistry, paraPrList));

    return zip;
  }

  public async renderToUint8Array(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    const zip = await this.buildZip(doc);
    return await zip.generateAsync({
      type: "uint8array",
      compression: "DEFLATE",
      compressionOptions: { level: 6 },
    });
  }

  public async renderToBlob(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Blob> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return new Blob([uint8 as any], {
      type: "application/hwp+zip",
    });
  }

  public async renderToBuffer(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Buffer> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return Buffer.from(uint8.buffer, uint8.byteOffset, uint8.byteLength);
  }

  private buildVersionXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hv:HCFVersion xmlns:hv="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/version" tagetApplication="WORDPROCESSOR" major="5" minor="1" micro="0" buildNumber="1" os="1" xmlVersion="1.2" application="Hancom Office Hangul" appVersion="10, 0, 0, 7888 WIN32LEWindows_8"/>`.trim();
  }

  private buildSettingsXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<ha:HWPApplicationSetting xmlns:ha="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/app">
  <ha:CaretPosition listIDRef="0" paraIDRef="0" pos="0"/>
</ha:HWPApplicationSetting>`.trim();
  }

  private buildContainerXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<container xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container" version="1.0">
  <rootfiles>
    <rootfile full-path="Contents/content.hpf" media-type="application/hwpml-package+xml"/>
  </rootfiles>
</container>`.trim();
  }

  private buildManifestXml(
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>
  ): string {
    let items = `  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="/" manifest:media-type="application/hwp+zip"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/content.hpf" manifest:media-type="application/hwpml-package+xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/header.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/section0.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="settings.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>`;

    for (const { binId, ext, mimeType } of imageBinMap.values()) {
      items += `\n  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="BinData/${binId}.${ext}" manifest:media-type="${mimeType}"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<manifest:manifest xmlns:manifest="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:manifest:1.0">
${items}
</manifest:manifest>`.trim();
  }

  private buildContentHpf(
    doc: CanvasDocument,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>
  ): string {
    let binItems = "";
    for (const { binId, ext, mimeType } of imageBinMap.values()) {
      binItems += `\n    <opf:item id="${binId}" href="BinData/${binId}.${ext}" media-type="${mimeType}" isEmbeded="1"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<opf:package xmlns:opf="http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf/" version="1.2" unique-identifier="bookid">
  <opf:metadata>
    <opf:title>${escapeXml(doc.title)}</opf:title>
    <opf:language>ko</opf:language>
  </opf:metadata>
  <opf:manifest>
    <opf:item id="header" href="Contents/header.xml" media-type="application/xml"/>
    <opf:item id="section0" href="Contents/section0.xml" media-type="application/xml"/>${binItems}
  </opf:manifest>
  <opf:spine>
    <opf:itemref idref="header"/>
    <opf:itemref idref="section0"/>
  </opf:spine>
</opf:package>`.trim();
  }

  private buildHeaderXml(
    charPrList: { id: number; key: CharPrKey }[],
    fontRegistry: Map<string, number>,
    paraPrList: ParaPrEntry[]
  ): string {
    let charPrEntries = "";
    for (const { id, key } of charPrList) {
      const heightHwpu = Math.round(key.fontSizePt * 100);
      const boldAttr = key.bold ? ' bold="1"' : "";
      const italicAttr = key.italic ? ' italic="1"' : "";
      const ls = key.letterSpacingPct || 0;
      const underlineXml = key.underline
        ? `\n      <hh:underline type="BOTTOM" shape="SOLID" color="#${key.colorHex}"/>`
        : "";
      const strikeXml = key.strike
        ? `\n      <hh:strikeout shape="CONTINUOUS" color="#${key.colorHex}"/>`
        : "";
      charPrEntries += `\n    <hh:charPr id="${id}" height="${heightHwpu}" textColor="#${key.colorHex}" shadeColor="none" useFontSpace="1" useKerning="1" symMark="NONE" borderFillIDRef="1"${boldAttr}${italicAttr}>
      <hh:fontRef hangul="${key.fontId}" latin="${key.fontId}" hanja="${key.fontId}" japanese="${key.fontId}" other="${key.fontId}" symbol="${key.fontId}" user="${key.fontId}"/>
      <hh:ratio hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:spacing hangul="${ls}" latin="${ls}" hanja="${ls}" japanese="${ls}" other="${ls}" symbol="${ls}" user="${ls}"/>
      <hh:relSz hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:offset hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>${underlineXml}${strikeXml}
    </hh:charPr>`;
    }

    if (charPrList.length === 0) {
      charPrEntries = `\n    <hh:charPr id="0" height="1000" textColor="#000000" shadeColor="none" useFontSpace="1" useKerning="1" symMark="NONE" borderFillIDRef="1">
      <hh:fontRef hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>
    </hh:charPr>`;
    }

    const paraPrEntries = paraPrList
      .map(
        (p) => `      <hh:paraPr id="${p.id}" tabPrIDRef="0" condense="0" fontLineHeight="0" snapToGrid="1" suppressLineNumbers="0" checked="0">
        <hh:align horizontal="${p.horizontal}" vertical="BASELINE"/>
        <hh:lineSpacing type="${p.spacingType}" value="${p.spacingValue}" unit="${p.spacingUnit}"/>
      </hh:paraPr>`
      )
      .join("\n");

    const langs = ["hangul", "latin", "hanja", "japanese", "other", "symbol", "user"];
    const fontCnt = fontRegistry.size;
    const fontXmlEntries = Array.from(fontRegistry.entries())
      .map(([face, id]) => `<hh:font id="${id}" face="${escapeXml(face)}" type="TTF" isEmbedded="0"/>`)
      .join("\n        ");

    const fontfaceBlocks = langs
      .map(
        (lang) => `<hh:fontface lang="${lang}" fontCnt="${fontCnt}">
        ${fontXmlEntries}
      </hh:fontface>`
      )
      .join("\n      ");

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hh:head xmlns:hh="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/head" xmlns:hc="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/core" version="1.0">
  <hh:beginNum page="1" footnote="1" endnote="1" pic="1" tbl="1" equation="1"/>
  <hh:refList>
    <hh:fontfaces itemCnt="7">
      ${fontfaceBlocks}
    </hh:fontfaces>
    <hh:borderFills itemCnt="1">
      <hh:borderFill id="1" threeD="0" shadow="0" centerLine="NONE" breakCellSeparateLine="0">
        <hh:slash type="NONE" Crooked="0" isCounter="0"/>
        <hh:backSlash type="NONE" Crooked="0" isCounter="0"/>
        <hh:leftBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:rightBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:topBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:bottomBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:diagonal type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="none" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>
      </hh:borderFill>
    </hh:borderFills>
    <hh:charProperties itemCnt="${Math.max(1, charPrList.length)}">${charPrEntries}
    </hh:charProperties>
    <hh:paraProperties itemCnt="${paraPrList.length}">
${paraPrEntries}
    </hh:paraProperties>
    <hh:styles itemCnt="1">
      <hh:style id="0" type="PARA" name="바탕글" engName="Normal" paraPrIDRef="0" charPrIDRef="0" nextStyleIDRef="0"/>
    </hh:styles>
  </hh:refList>
</hh:head>`.trim();
  }

  private buildSection0Xml(
    doc: CanvasDocument,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>,
    getCharPrId: (run: CanvasTextRun) => number,
    getParaPrId: (align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify", lineHeightPt?: number) => number
  ): string {
    const pageParas: string[] = [];
    let globalShapeId = 1;

    for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < doc.pages.length; pIdx++) {
      const page = doc.pages[pIdx];
      const pageBreakAttr = pIdx === 0 ? ' pageBreak="0"' : ' pageBreak="1"';

      // Dynamic page size and orientation (per page)
      const pageWidthPt = page.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt || 595.28;
      const pageHeightPt = page.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt || 841.89;
      const isLandscape = pageWidthPt > pageHeightPt;
      const landscapeAttr = isLandscape ? "WIDELY" : "NARROWLY";
      const widthHwpu = Math.round(pageWidthPt * 100);
      const heightHwpu = Math.round(pageHeightPt * 100);

      const shapesXml: string[] = [];

      // 1. Full-page background rect if specified (sized to this page's dimensions)
      if (page.backgroundColor) {
        const bgW = ptToHwpu(pageWidthPt);
        const bgH = ptToHwpu(pageHeightPt);
        const bgId = globalShapeId++;
        shapesXml.push(
          `<hp:run><hp:rect id="${bgId}" zOrder="0" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${bgId}" ratio="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${bgW}" height="${bgH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${bgW}" height="${bgH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(bgW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(bgH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="0" vertOffset="0"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="#000000" width="0" style="NONE" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(page.backgroundColor)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>
</hp:rect></hp:run>`
        );
      }

      // 2. Render all elements
      for (const elem of page.elements) {
        const rendered = this.renderElement(
          globalShapeId++,
          elem,
          imageBinMap,
          getCharPrId,
          getParaPrId,
          doc.resources
        );
        if (rendered) {
          shapesXml.push(`<hp:run>${rendered}</hp:run>`);
        }
      }

      // First page includes secPr, as well as any page where dimensions/orientation differ from previous
      const prevPage = pIdx > 0 ? doc.pages[pIdx - 1] : undefined;
      const prevWidthPt = prevPage ? (prevPage.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt) : undefined;
      const prevHeightPt = prevPage ? (prevPage.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt) : undefined;
      const orientationChanged =
        prevWidthPt !== undefined &&
        (Math.abs(prevWidthPt - pageWidthPt) > 1 || Math.abs(prevHeightPt! - pageHeightPt) > 1);

      const secPrXml =
        pIdx === 0 || orientationChanged
          ? `<hp:secPr id="${pIdx}" textDirection="HORIZONTAL" spaceColumns="1134" tabStop="8000" outlineShapeIDRef="0" memoShapeIDRef="0" textVerticalWidthHead="0">
  <hp:grid char="0" line="0"/>
  <hp:startNum pageStartsOn="BOTH" page="${pIdx + 1}" pic="1" tbl="1" equation="1"/>
  <hp:visibility hideFirstHeader="0" hideFirstFooter="0" hideFirstMasterPage="0" border="SHOW_ALL" fill="SHOW_ALL" hideFirstPageNum="0" hideFirstEmptyLine="0" showLineNumber="0"/>
  <hp:pagePr landscape="${landscapeAttr}" width="${widthHwpu}" height="${heightHwpu}" gutterType="LEFT_ONLY">
    <hp:margin header="0" footer="0" gutter="0" left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
  </hp:pagePr>
</hp:secPr>`
          : "";

      pageParas.push(`<hp:p id="${pIdx}" paraPrIDRef="0" styleIDRef="0"${pageBreakAttr} columnBreak="0" merged="0">
  ${secPrXml}
  ${shapesXml.join("\n  ")}
</hp:p>`);
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hs:sec xmlns:hs="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/section"
        xmlns:hp="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/paragraph"
        xmlns:hc="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/core">
  ${pageParas.join("\n  ")}
</hs:sec>`.trim();
  }

  private renderElement(
    shapeId: number,
    elem: CanvasElement,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>,
    getCharPrId: (run: CanvasTextRun) => number,
    getParaPrId: (align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify", lineHeightPt?: number) => number,
    resources?: Record<string, CanvasResource>
  ): string {
    const xHwpu = ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.x);
    const yHwpu = ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.y);
    const wHwpu = Math.max(100, ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.width));
    const hHwpu = Math.max(100, ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.height));
    const zOrder = elem.zIndex;

    // 1. Image with FILL Cover Crop, Explicit CROP, or FIT Letterbox
    if (elem.type === "image" && elem.imageRef) {
      const binEntry = imageBinMap.get(elem.imageRef);
      if (binEntry) {
        const binId = binEntry.binId;
        const res = resources?.[elem.imageRef];
        let imgW = 0;
        let imgH = 0;

        if (res && res.data) {
          const d = res.data;
          const readU16 = (off: number) => ((d[off] || 0) << 8) | (d[off + 1] || 0);
          const readU32 = (off: number) =>
            (((d[off] || 0) << 24) >>> 0) +
            ((d[off + 1] || 0) << 16) +
            ((d[off + 2] || 0) << 8) +
            (d[off + 3] || 0);

          if (d.length >= 24 && d[0] === 0x89 && d[1] === 0x50 && d[2] === 0x4e && d[3] === 0x47) {
            imgW = readU32(16);
            imgH = readU32(20);
          } else if (d.length >= 4 && d[0] === 0xff && d[1] === 0xd8) {
            let offset = 2;
            while (offset < d.length - 8) {
              const marker = readU16(offset);
              offset += 2;
              if (marker === 0xffc0 || marker === 0xffc2) {
                imgH = readU16(offset + 3);
                imgW = readU16(offset + 5);
                break;
              }
              const len = readU16(offset);
              offset += len;
            }
          }
        }

        let picX = xHwpu;
        let picY = yHwpu;
        let picW = wHwpu;
        let picH = hHwpu;
        let dimW = imgW > 0 ? Math.round(imgW * 75) : wHwpu;
        let dimH = imgH > 0 ? Math.round(imgH * 75) : hHwpu;
        let clipLeft = 0;
        let clipRight = dimW;
        let clipTop = 0;
        let clipBottom = dimH;

        if (
          elem.imageCrop &&
          (elem.imageCrop.top > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.bottom > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.left > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.right > 0)
        ) {
          const cLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.left));
          const cRight = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.right));
          const cTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.top));
          const cBottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.bottom));
          clipLeft = Math.round(dimW * cLeft);
          clipRight = Math.round(dimW * (1 - cRight));
          clipTop = Math.round(dimH * cTop);
          clipBottom = Math.round(dimH * (1 - cBottom));
        } else if (elem.imageScaleMode === "FIT" && imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;
          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            picH = Math.max(100, Math.round(wHwpu / arImg));
            picY = Math.round(yHwpu + (hHwpu - picH) / 2);
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            picW = Math.max(100, Math.round(hHwpu * arImg));
            picX = Math.round(xHwpu + (wHwpu - picW) / 2);
          }
          clipLeft = 0;
          clipRight = dimW;
          clipTop = 0;
          clipBottom = dimH;
        } else if (imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;

          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            // Image is wider than container: crop left & right
            const visW = dimH * arBox;
            const cropX = (dimW - visW) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = Math.round(cropX);
            clipRight = Math.round(dimW - cropX);
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            // Image is taller than container: crop top & bottom
            const visH = dimW / arBox;
            const cropY = (dimH - visH) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = Math.round(cropY);
            clipBottom = Math.round(dimH - cropY);
          } else {
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          }
        }

        return `<hp:pic id="${shapeId}" zOrder="${zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${shapeId}" ratio="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${picW}" height="${picH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${picW}" height="${picH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(picW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(picH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="${picX}" vertOffset="${picY}"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="#000000" width="0" style="NONE" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:imgRect pt0_x="0" pt0_y="0" pt1_x="${picW}" pt1_y="0" pt2_x="${picW}" pt2_y="${picH}" pt3_x="0" pt3_y="${picH}"/>
  <hp:imgClip left="${clipLeft}" right="${clipRight}" top="${clipTop}" bottom="${clipBottom}"/>
  <hp:imgDim dimwidth="${dimW}" dimheight="${dimH}"/>
  <hp:inImgDim dimwidth="${dimW}" dimheight="${dimH}"/>
  <hc:img binaryItemIDRef="${binId}" binRef="${binId}" bright="0" contrast="0" effect="REAL_PIC" alpha="0"/>
</hp:pic>`;
      }
    }

    // 2. Line (KS X 6101 native hp:line)
    if (elem.type === "line") {
      const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
      const strokeW = Math.max(10, Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10)).toString();
      const strokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
      const lineShapeW = Math.max(1, wHwpu);
      const lineShapeH = Math.max(0, hHwpu);
      return `<hp:line id="${shapeId}" zOrder="${zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${shapeId}" isReverseHWP97="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${lineShapeW}" height="${lineShapeH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${lineShapeW}" height="${lineShapeH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(lineShapeW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(lineShapeH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="${xHwpu}" vertOffset="${yHwpu}"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${strokeColor}" width="${strokeW}" style="${strokeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:pt1 x="${lineShapeW}" y="${lineShapeH}"/>
</hp:line>`;
    }

    // 3. Card / Rect / Textbox
    const isText = elem.type === "text" || (elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);
    const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor;
    const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

    const lineStyle = hasStroke ? (elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID") : "NONE";
    const lineColor = hasStroke ? `#${cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor)}` : "#000000";
    const lineWidth = hasStroke ? Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10).toString() : "0";

    const fillBrush = hasFill
      ? `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`
      : `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="none" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`;

    const shapeW = wHwpu;
    const shapeH = hHwpu;

    let drawTextPart = "";
    if (isText && elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0) {
      const firstLineHeightPt = elem.textRuns.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt && r.lineHeightPt > 0)?.lineHeightPt;
      const paraPrId = getParaPrId(elem.paragraphAlign, firstLineHeightPt);

      // Split runs across lines into separate paragraphs
      const paragraphs: Array<Array<{ charPrId: number; text: string }>> = [[]];

      for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
        const charPrId = getCharPrId(run);
        const lines = run.text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          if (i > 0) {
            paragraphs.push([]);
          }
          if (lines[i].length > 0 || lines.length === 1) {
            paragraphs[paragraphs.length - 1].push({ charPrId, text: lines[i] });
          }
        }
      }

      const parasXml: string[] = paragraphs.map((paraRuns, pIdx) => {
        const runsStr =
          paraRuns.length > 0
            ? paraRuns
                .map((r) => `<hp:run charPrIDRef="${r.charPrId}"><hp:t>${escapeXml(r.text)}</hp:t></hp:run>`)
                .join("")
            : `<hp:run charPrIDRef="0"><hp:t/></hp:run>`;
        return `<hp:p id="${pIdx}" paraPrIDRef="${paraPrId}" styleIDRef="0" pageBreak="0" columnBreak="0" merged="0">${runsStr}</hp:p>`;
      });

      const drawName = escapeXml(elem.name || elem.id || "");
      const vertAlignAttr =
        elem.verticalAlign === "center" ? "CENTER" : elem.verticalAlign === "bottom" ? "BOTTOM" : "TOP";

      const textMarginXml = elem.innerPaddingPt
        ? `\n    <hp:textMargin left="${ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.left ?? 0)}" right="${ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.right ?? 0)}" top="${ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.top ?? 0)}" bottom="${ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.bottom ?? 0)}"/>`
        : "";

      drawTextPart = `
  <hp:drawText lastWidth="${shapeW}" name="${drawName}" editable="1">${textMarginXml}
    <hp:subList id="" textDirection="HORIZONTAL" lineWrap="SQUEEZE" vertAlign="${vertAlignAttr}" linkListIDRef="0" linkListNextIDRef="0" textWidth="${shapeW}" textHeight="${shapeH}" hasTextRef="0" hasNumRef="0">
      ${parasXml.join("\n      ")}
    </hp:subList>
  </hp:drawText>`;
    }

    // 4. Vector Shape (KS X 6101 native hp:polygon for closed paths / hp:line for open subpaths)
    if (elem.type === "vector") {
      let subpaths = elem.vectorSubpaths;
      if (!subpaths || subpaths.length === 0) {
        if (elem.rawSvgPath) {
          subpaths = SvgPath.parse(elem.rawSvgPath).toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(shapeW, shapeH);
        } else if (elem.vectorPoints && elem.vectorPoints.length > 0) {
          subpaths = [{ points: elem.vectorPoints, closed: true }];
        }
      }

      if (subpaths && subpaths.length > 0) {
        const renderedShapes: string[] = [];
        for (let idx = 0; idx < subpaths.length; idx++) {
          const sp = subpaths[idx];
          const subId = `${shapeId}_${idx + 1}`;
          const pts = sp.points;

          // Open subpath: emit connected <hp:line> segments so Hancom does NOT draw a closing stroke or fill!
          if (!sp.closed && pts.length >= 2) {
            const openStrokeColor = hasStroke
              ? lineColor
              : hasFill
              ? `#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}`
              : "#000000";
            const openStrokeWidth = hasStroke ? lineWidth : "15";
            const openStrokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
            const endCap = pts.length > 2 ? "ROUND" : "FLAT";

            for (let segIdx = 0; segIdx < pts.length - 1; segIdx++) {
              const p0 = pts[segIdx];
              const p1 = pts[segIdx + 1];
              const segId = pts.length === 2 ? subId : `${subId}_${segIdx + 1}`;
              renderedShapes.push(`<hp:line id="${segId}" zOrder="${zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${segId}" isReverseHWP97="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(shapeW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(shapeH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="${xHwpu}" vertOffset="${yHwpu}"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="${openStrokeColor}" width="${openStrokeWidth}" style="${openStrokeStyle}" endCap="${endCap}" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:pt0 x="${p0.x}" y="${p0.y}"/>
  <hp:pt1 x="${p1.x}" y="${p1.y}"/>
</hp:line>`);
            }
            continue;
          }

          const ptXml =
            pts.length > 0
              ? pts.map((p) => `<hp:pt x="${p.x}" y="${p.y}"/>`).join("\n  ")
              : `<hp:pt x="0" y="0"/>\n  <hp:pt x="${shapeW}" y="0"/>\n  <hp:pt x="${shapeW}" y="${shapeH}"/>\n  <hp:pt x="0" y="${shapeH}"/>`;

          const subFillBrush =
            sp.closed && hasFill
              ? fillBrush
              : `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="none" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`;

          renderedShapes.push(`<hp:polygon id="${subId}" zOrder="${zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${subId}" ratio="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(shapeW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(shapeH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="${xHwpu}" vertOffset="${yHwpu}"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="${lineColor}" width="${lineWidth}" style="${lineStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${subFillBrush}
  ${ptXml}${idx === 0 ? drawTextPart : ""}
</hp:polygon>`);
        }

        return renderedShapes.join("\n</hp:run>\n<hp:run>");
      }
    }

    const isOval = elem.type === "oval";
    const shapeTag = isOval ? "hp:oval" : "hp:rect";
    const cornerRadius = elem.style?.cornerRadius || 0;
    const minDim = Math.min(elem.bounds.width, elem.bounds.height);
    const ratio =
      !isOval && cornerRadius > 0 && minDim > 0
        ? Math.min(50, Math.max(0, Math.round((cornerRadius / (minDim / 2)) * 50)))
        : 0;

    return `<${shapeTag} id="${shapeId}" zOrder="${zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${shapeId}" ratio="${ratio}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${shapeW}" height="${shapeH}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(shapeW / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(shapeH / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzAlign="LEFT" vertAlign="TOP" horzOffset="${xHwpu}" vertOffset="${yHwpu}"/>
  <hp:lineShape color="${lineColor}" width="${lineWidth}" style="${lineStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${fillBrush}${drawTextPart}
</${shapeTag}>`;
  }
}

File: src/canvas/utils/binaryUtils.ts

/**
 * Cross-platform binary utilities for Canvas IR.
 * Safe for both Node.js and Browser environments (no direct Buffer global requirement).
 */

export function decodeBase64(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  // If Buffer is available in the environment (e.g. Node.js), use it for speed
  if (typeof Buffer !== "undefined" && typeof Buffer.from === "function") {
    return new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(b64, "base64"));
  }
  // Otherwise, use browser atob
  const binary = atob(b64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff;
  }
  return bytes;
}

export function decodeDataUrl(source: string): { mime?: string; bytes: Uint8Array } | null {
  if (!source.startsWith("data:")) return null;
  const comma = source.indexOf(",");
  if (comma < 0) return null;

  const meta = source.slice(5, comma);
  const payload = source.slice(comma + 1).replace(/\s/g, "");
  if (!payload) return null;

  const mime = meta.split(";")[0] || undefined;
  try {
    return { mime, bytes: decodeBase64(payload) };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}


================================================================================
File: src/canvas/utils/imageResolver.ts
================================================================================
/**
 * Image URL resolution utility for Canvas IR.
 * Converts remote Figma image fill URLs (e.g. AWS S3) into Base64 Data URLs
 * for offline embedding in DOCX and HWPX document archives.
 */

export async function resolveImageMapUrls(
  imageMap: Record<string, string>,
  downloadBase64: (url: string) => Promise<string>
): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  const resolved: Record<string, string> = {};
  const entries = Object.entries(imageMap);

  await Promise.all(
    entries.map(async ([ref, url]) => {
      if (!url) return;
      if (url.startsWith("http://") || url.startsWith("https://")) {
        try {
          const dataUrl = await downloadBase64(url);
          resolved[ref] = dataUrl;
        } catch {
          // Keep original or fallback
          resolved[ref] = url;
        }
      } else {
        resolved[ref] = url;
      }
    })
  );

  return resolved;
}


================================================================================
File: tests/phase19-docx-text-fidelity.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  DocxCanvasRenderer,
  FigmaCanvasParser,
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasElement,
} from "../src/canvas/index.js";

describe("Phase 19: DOCX Text Fidelity Finalization Suite", () => {
  const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();

  it("Item 1: Fixed-width Korean text automatically wraps and sets mso-wrap-style:square", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Fixed Width Korean Wrap",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {},
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          elements: [
            {
              id: "txt_wrap_korean",
              name: "Korean Paragraph",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 40, y: 40, width: 200, height: 60 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [
                {
                  text: "주의: 본 균열 폭 측정 결과는 GSD 기준의 AI 영상 분석 추정치이며 물리적 정밀 측정을 요합니다.",
                  fontFamily: "맑은 고딕",
                  fontSizePt: 10,
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  color: "#1F2937",
                  lineHeightPt: 14,
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must have mso-wrap-style:square to enforce WordWrap in Word COM
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-wrap-style:square");
    expect(xml).not.toContain('id="s_0_0"[^>]*mso-wrap-style:none');
    expect(xml).toContain("주의: 본 균열 폭 측정 결과는");
  });

  it("Item 2: Explicit line breaks are strictly preserved as <w:br/>", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Explicit Breaks",

  });

  it("Item 3: Auto-width text (WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT) preserves single-line rendering with mso-wrap-style:none to prevent unwanted wrapping and clipping", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ scalePolicy: "fit-a4" });
    const figmaJson = {
      id: "root",
      name: "Auto Width Frame",
      type: "FRAME",
      absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
      children: [
        {
          id: "txt_auto_wh",
          name: "Auto Width Text",
          type: "TEXT",
          characters: "단일 행 라벨 (Single Line)",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 180, height: 24 },
          style: {
            fontFamily: "맑은 고딕",
            fontSize: 14,
            textAutoResize: "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT",
          },
        },
      ],
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(figmaJson as any);
    const el = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements[0];
    expect(el.textAutoResize).toBe("WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT");

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Single-line auto-width text must NOT auto-wrap to prevent Word font variance clipping
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-wrap-style:none");
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-fit-shape-to-text:t");
  });

  it("Item 5: Tight line-height triggers atLeast lineRule to prevent Korean/English descender clipping", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Descender LineRule",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },

        {
          pageNumber: 1,

              id: "txt_tight_descender",

                },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);

    expect(xml).toContain("mso-fit-shape-to-text:t");
  });

  it("Item 10: Non-text elements (backgrounds, lines, images) preserve mso-wrap-style:none", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Non-Text Preservation",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {
        "dot-img": {
          id: "dot-img",
          name: "dot.png",
          mimeType: "image/png",
          data: Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mNk+M9QDwADhgGAWjR9awAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==", "base64"),
        },
      },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          elements: [
            {
              id: "dec_line",
              name: "Divider Line",
              type: "line",
              bounds: { x: 40, y: 100, width: 500, height: 0 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: { strokeColor: "#E5E7EB", strokeWidth: 1 },
            },
            {
              id: "dec_card",
              name: "Empty Card",
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: 40, y: 120, width: 200, height: 100 },
              zIndex: 2,
              style: { fillColor: "#F9FAFB", strokeColor: "#E5E7EB", strokeWidth: 1 },
            },
            {
              id: "dec_img",

              zIndex: 3,

    };

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Empty card without text must have mso-wrap-style:none
    expect(xml).toContain('id="s_0_1"');

    expect(xml).not.toContain('<v:textbox');
  });

  it("Item 11: Single-line labels with HEIGHT resize and estimatedLines < 1.4 preserve mso-wrap-style:none, while multi-line paragraphs wrap with mso-wrap-style:square", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Precision Wrapping Separation",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {},
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          elements: [
            // Single-line cell (HEIGHT resize from auto-layout, but 1-line tall)
            {
              id: "single_cell_height",
              name: "부산 해운대구 좌동 1407",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 150, height: 11 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textAutoResize: "HEIGHT",
              textRuns: [
                {
                  text: "부산 해운대구 좌동 1407",
                  fontFamily: "맑은 고딕",
                  fontSizePt: 8.5,
                  lineHeightPt: 10.6,
                },
              ],
            },
            // Multi-line paragraph (HEIGHT resize, 5 lines tall)
            {
              id: "multi_paragraph_height",
              name: "Exterior Wall Inspection Summary",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 100, width: 450, height: 75 },
              zIndex: 2,
              style: {},
              textAutoResize: "HEIGHT",
              textRuns: [
                {
                  text: "해운대동신아파트(해동신) 1차 외벽 균열 진단 결과, 단지 전체 CDI는 0.031%로 양호한 수준입니다. 다만 109동과 108동은 상대적으로 균열 밀도가 높아 조기 보수를 권장합니다. 특히 109동 5층 A면의 수직 균열(폭 0.82cm)은 구조적 영향 가능성이 있으므로 정밀 조사를 병행할 것을 제안합니다.",
                  fontFamily: "맑은 고딕",
                  fontSizePt: 9.5,
                  lineHeightPt: 14,
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Single-line cell must have mso-wrap-style:none to stay on 1 line
    expect(xml).toMatch(/alt="single_cell_height"[^>]*style="[^"]*mso-wrap-style:none/);

    // Multi-line paragraph must have mso-wrap-style:square to wrap inside 450pt
    expect(xml).toMatch(/alt="multi_paragraph_height"[^>]*style="[^"]*mso-wrap-style:square/);
  });

  it("Item 12: individualStrokeWeights renders single line dividers instead of 4-sided boxes", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ scalePolicy: "fit-a4" });
    const figmaJson = {
      id: "root",
      name: "Page with Dividers",
      type: "FRAME",
      absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
      children: [
        // Page header: bottom border only
        {
          id: "header_divider",
          name: "PageHeader",
          type: "FRAME",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 20, width: 694, height: 20 },
          strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.8, g: 0.8, b: 0.8, a: 1 } }],
          strokeWeight: 1,
          individualStrokeWeights: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 1, left: 0 },
          children: [],
        },
        // Page footer: top border only
        {
          id: "footer_divider",
          name: "PageFooter",
          type: "FRAME",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 1080, width: 694, height: 25 },
          strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.8, g: 0.8, b: 0.8, a: 1 } }],
          strokeWeight: 1,
          individualStrokeWeights: { top: 1, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
          children: [],
        },
        // Table cell: background fill with left border divider
        {
          id: "cell_left_border",
          name: "TableCell",
          type: "FRAME",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 200, y: 100, width: 120, height: 40 },
          fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.95, g: 0.95, b: 0.95, a: 1 } }],
          strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.7, g: 0.7, b: 0.7, a: 1 } }],
          strokeWeight: 1,
          individualStrokeWeights: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 1 },
          children: [],
        },
        // Zero stroke weight frame: should NOT render any stroke
        {
          id: "zero_stroke_frame",
          name: "ZeroStrokeFrame",
          type: "FRAME",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 200, width: 200, height: 40 },
          strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.5, g: 0.5, b: 0.5, a: 1 } }],
          strokeWeight: 0,
          children: [],
        },
      ],
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(figmaJson as any);
    const elements = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements;

    // Header: must emit a single line at bottom, no 4-sided card
    const headerLines = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "header_divider" && e.type === "line");
    const headerCards = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "header_divider" && e.type === "card");
    expect(headerLines.length).toBe(1);
    expect(headerCards.length).toBe(0);

    // Footer: must emit a single line at top, no 4-sided card
    const footerLines = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "footer_divider" && e.type === "line");
    const footerCards = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "footer_divider" && e.type === "card");
    expect(footerLines.length).toBe(1);
    expect(footerCards.length).toBe(0);

    // Table cell: must emit 1 fill card (fill-only) and 1 left line divider
    const cellCards = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "cell_left_border" && e.type === "card");
    const cellLines = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "cell_left_border" && e.type === "line");
    expect(cellCards.length).toBe(1);
    expect(cellCards[0].style.strokeColor).toBeUndefined();
    expect(cellLines.length).toBe(1);

    // Zero stroke frame: no card and no line
    const zeroElements = elements.filter((e) => e.sourceNodeId === "zero_stroke_frame");
    expect(zeroElements.length).toBe(0);

    // Verify DOCX rendering output
    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Header & footer must render as <v:line>
    expect(xml).toContain('o:title="PageHeader_bottom"');
    expect(xml).toContain('o:title="PageFooter_top"');
    expect(xml).toContain('o:title="TableCell_left"');
  });

  it("Item 13: Overlaid text in container badges and cells is vertically centered with v-text-anchor:middle", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ scalePolicy: "fit-a4" });
    const figmaJson = {
      id: "root",
      name: "Badge Page",
      type: "FRAME",
      absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
      children: [
        {
          id: "badge_container",
          name: "StatusBadge",
          type: "FRAME",
          absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 100, y: 100, width: 80, height: 28 },
          layoutMode: "HORIZONTAL",
          paddingLeft: 12,
          paddingRight: 12,
          paddingTop: 6,
          paddingBottom: 6,
          fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.2, g: 0.3, b: 0.8, a: 1 } }],
          children: [
            {
              id: "badge_text",
              name: "1차 진단",
              type: "TEXT",
              characters: "1차 진단",
              absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 112, y: 106, width: 56, height: 16 },
              style: {
                fontFamily: "Inter",
                fontSize: 13,
                textAlignVertical: "TOP",
                textAutoResize: "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT",
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(figmaJson as any);
    const textElem = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements.find((e) => e.id === "badge_text")!;
    expect(textElem).toBeDefined();
    // Must be detected as vertically centered
    expect(textElem.verticalAlign).toBe("center");
    // Height must span the parent container
    expect(textElem.bounds.height).toBeCloseTo(28 * (595.28 / 794), 1);
    expect(textElem.contentHeightPt).toBeCloseTo(16 * (595.28 / 794), 1);

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must have v-text-anchor:middle and mso-fit-shape-to-text:f to preserve vertical centering
    expect(xml).toContain("v-text-anchor:middle");
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-fit-shape-to-text:f");
  });
});


================================================================================
File: tests/phase18-architecture-fidelity.test.ts
